Universal-indicator comparator · practical record

Science Atlas360° · VisionariesAI Labs Private Limited

Aim

Compare a particular five-dye mixture with a matched-concentration constituent reference,account for finite doses and distinguish colour evidence from independently imposed pH.

Student hypothesis and plan

Account for stock,pipette and cell separately.

Apparatus,controlled variables and procedure

  1. Aim: compare a particular broad-range five-dye indicator mixture with one constituent at the same independently imposed reference pH.
  2. Apparatus: two independent prepared stock reservoirs,two metered pipettes,two equal reference cells,a white comparison stage,selected mixture teaching cards and a constituent transition-range panel. This is a virtual comparator,not a copied commercial colour chart.
  3. Record the stock preparation volume,equal initial cell volumes,planned indicator doses,selected constituent and imposed reference pH. The single-constituent stock has the same concentration of that constituent as the five-dye mixture.
  4. Scale the particular reference formulation to the selected final stock volume. Keep the five mixture components and the independent single-component stock inventories separate. A particular formulation does not represent every commercial product.
  5. Aspirate the selected mixture aliquot into its own pipette,then move the loaded tip and dispense into its own cell. Moving a loaded pipette does not put that liquid into the cell. Record actual delivered fraction,volume,all five dye masses and stock remaining. Complete the selected dose before reading the illustrative card.
  6. Aspirate the chosen constituent from its own stock into its own pipette,then dispense into the second cell. With both equal doses complete,the selected constituent amount is the same in both cells. Partial unequal doses must be recorded as unequal instead of silently normalized.
  7. Record the mixture teaching category and the constituent endpoint. Within a stated constituent transition range,mark the intermediate hue unresolved. A completed colourless phenolphthalein reference still contains its measured indicator dose.
  8. Calculate additive cell volumes,nominal solvent fractions,transferred dye amounts,remaining stock amounts and cell mass concentrations. Keep grams,litres,milligrams,millilitres and microlitres explicit. Retain stock and cell closure checks.
  9. Optional ideal H⁺/OH⁻ calculations use the independently maintained pH at25°C,not the observed shade. Compare H⁺ with the pH7reference using the logarithmic ratio. Do not infer exact pKa from a transition midpoint or calculate unprovided optical absorbance.
  10. Write the result and conclusion separately from the numerical assumptions. State that selected mixture cards are illustrative,real buffer/indicator loading and solvent effects are omitted,and colour matching does not prove exact pH or chemical identity.

Limitations
